2026年 10月 30日 全5頁

10月会合で日銀は政策金利を据え置き 
2％の物価安定に向け利上げ継続へ 
利上げの悪影響は今のところ限定的、12月に1.5％へ追加利上げか

経済調査部 チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司 
  エコノミスト 秋元 虹輝

[要約] 
◼ 日本銀行（日銀）は2026年10月29、30日の金融政策決定会合で、短期金利を1.25％
程度に据え置いた。日銀の政策運営は、基調的な物価上昇率を「2％に近づける」局面
から「2％程度で安定させる」局面に移った。今後は、経済・物価・金融情勢に応じて
政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく方針だ。 
◼ 日銀は2024年 3月以降、金融政策の正常化を進めてきたが、企業・家計への悪影響は
これまでのところ限定的だ。企業では比較的良好な資金調達環境やネット有利子負債
比率の低下が、家計では収入の増加や借入返済額の減少に加え、住宅ローン金利上昇
の返済額への反映の遅れが、足元の負担増を抑えている。ただし、家計の借入残高は
収入対比で高水準にあり、金利高が長期化すれば、返済負担が徐々に高まる可能性が
ある。 
◼ 当社では、日銀は 2026年 12月会合で短期金利を1.5％、2027年 3月会合で 1.75％へ
引き上げた後、いったん据え置くとみている。ただし、その後も金融環境がなお緩和
的で物価上振れリスクが残れば、同年6月会合で2.0％への追加利上げに踏み切る可能
性がある。

2026年 10月会合で日銀は政策金利の据え置きを決定 
日本銀行（日銀）は2026年10月29、30日に開催された金融政策決定会合で、無担保コール
レート・オーバーナイト物（以下、短期金利）を 1.25％程度に据え置くことを決めた1。今回
は利上げを決定した翌月の会合ということもあり、これまでの利上げの影響を見極めることを
重視したとみられる。

1 日本銀行「当面の金融政策運営について」（2026年10月 30日）

### Page 2

1

2 / 5

連続利上げに慎重な姿勢は、前回会合後の総裁発言からもうかがえる。9月会合直後の記者
会見で植田和男総裁は、「大きな幅の利上げとか何回も連続して利上げするというようなこと
は、端的に申し上げれば、物価が目標を超えてものすごい上がっていくというリスクが高いか、
あるいは既に目標を超えちゃっているかというようなことに依存するんだと思います」2と述べ、
その例として2022、23年の米欧の高インフレ局面を挙げた。 
また、日銀が保有する上場投資信託（ETF）については、2026年 1月以降の売却が市場に大
きな影響を与えることなく円滑に進捗したことなどを踏まえ、2027年 1月から年間の売却ペー
スを簿価で 3,300億円程度から 8,600億円程度（市場全体の売買代金に占める年間売却額（時
価）の割合は0.05％程度から0.1％程度）に引き上げることを決めた3。

インフレ基調は2％目標と概ね整合的で、今後は定着に向けて利上げ継続へ 
9月会合の声明文で「2％に近づいている」4と評価した消費者物価の基調的な上昇率につい
ては、10月会合で公表された「経済・物価情勢の展望」（展望レポート）で「2％の『物価安定
の目標』と概ね整合的な水準」に修正された。さらに、「従来は、基調的な物価上昇率を 2％に
向けて引き上げていく過程にあり、物価の下振れリスクに相応の注意を払うことが必要であっ
たが、足もとでは局面が変化しており、今後は、基調的な物価上昇率が 2％を超えて上振れて
いくリスクが顕在化し、それがその後の経済に悪影響を及ぼすことがないよう、十分に留意す
る必要がある」と指摘した5。 
植田総裁は 9月会合直後の記者会見ですでに、基調的な物価上昇率を 2％程度で安定させて
いくことが重要になったとして、 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明していた。10月会合での文
言変更は、こうした政策局面の変化をより明確にしたものといえる。また、10月 16日の記者
会見でも、今後は基調的な物価上昇率が「これ以上上がるということは容認できない」6と述べ、
2％近辺での定着を目指す考えを改めて示した。 
展望レポートでは、引き続き物価上振れリスクへの警戒を示し、経済・物価・金融情勢に応
じて政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく方針を前回と同様に明記した。日
銀が物価上振れリスクを見極める上で注視しているのが、①中東情勢に伴う原油価格、②AI関
連需要、③為替相場の3つである。足元ではAI関連投資の収益性への懸念が広がり、円安も一
服しているものの、日銀はこれらの動向が国内物価にどの程度波及するかを引き続き見極めて
いくとみられる。 
展望レポートを見ると、2026年度の実質 GDP成長率は春先以降のエネルギー・原材料価格の
上昇を受けて伸び率を縮小しつつも緩やかな成長を続け、2027年度以降は所得から支出への前

2 日本銀行「総裁記者会見」（2026年9月18日、24日公表） 
3 日本銀行「ETFの売却ペースの見直しに関する決定（2026年 10月）」（2026年 10月30日） 
4 日本銀行「金融市場調節方針の変更について」（2026年9月 18日） 
5 日本銀行「経済・物価情勢の展望（2026年10月）」（基本的見解、2026年10月 30日） 
6 日本銀行「植田総裁記者会見(10月 16日) ―G20終了後の植田総裁、三村財務官 共同記者会見における総裁
発言」（2026年10月 20日）

### Page 3

1

3 / 5

向きな循環メカニズムが強まるもとで成長率を緩やかに高めていくとされた（政策委員見通し
の中央値は2026年度＋0.7％、27年度＋0.7％、28年度＋0.8％） 。 
一方、消費者物価（除く生鮮食品）の前年比は、原油価格の上昇や半導体価格等の上昇、為
替円安などを背景に見通し期間前半に 2％をはっきりと上回る水準まで伸び率を高めた後、後
半には原油高の影響の減衰により、 2％程度に向けてプラス幅を縮小する見通しだ（政策委員
見通しの中央値は 2026年度＋2.5％、2027年度＋2.6％、2028年度＋2.0％） 。夏場以降の原油
価格上昇の影響などから、2027年度が前回（7月）から 0.2％pt上方修正された。消費者物価
（除く生鮮食品・エネルギー）の見通しは 2026年度＋2.4％、2027年度＋2.7％、2028年度＋
2.3％と、前回からの修正は小幅にとどまった。なお、2027年 4月から実施される可能性のあ
る飲食料品の消費税率引き下げは、物価見通しに反映されていない。 
利上げが続く中でも企業・家計への影響はこれまでのところ限定的 
日銀は2024年3月の金融政策決定会合でマイナス金利政策の解除とイールドカーブ・コント
ロール（長短金利操作）の撤廃を決め、その後 5回の利上げや国債買入れの減額を実施するな
ど金融政策の正常化を進めてきた。 
もっとも、企業や家計の経済活動への悪影響はこれまでのところ限定的で、金融環境はなお
緩和的とみられる。まず企業部門を見ると、日銀短観の業況判断DIは高水準を維持しており、
資金繰り判断DIや金融機関の貸出態度判断DIにも目立った悪化は見られない（図表1左）。中
小企業でも顕著な悪化は見られず、利上げが進む中でも、企業の景況感や資金調達環境は総じ
て良好な状態にある。 
ただし、過去の利上げ局面との単純な比較には注意が必要だ。1990年頃までの利上げは、資
産バブル期を含む高成長・高インフレ環境の下で実施されたのに対し、 2000年代以降は低成
長・デフレないし低インフレが長く続き、企業の財務構造も大きく変化した。足元で利上げの
影響が表れにくい背景には、企業の財務体質の改善がある。 
有利子負債から現預金を控除した額の総資産に対する比率（ネット有利子負債比率）は、資
本金 1千万円以上 10億円未満の中小・中堅企業で歴史的な低水準にある（図表 1右）。大企業
では中小・中堅企業ほど低下しておらず、資本金 1千万円未満の企業では相対的に高いものの、
企業部門全体では 1970～90 年代に比べて大幅に低下している。こうした財務構造の変化によ
り、政策金利が上昇しても、企業部門全体では利払い費の増加を通じた金融引き締めの影響が
以前より表れにくくなっていると考えられる。

図表 1：日銀短観の各種 DIの長期推移（左） 、企業規模別に見たネット有利子負債比率（右）

（注）左図は全産業ベースで、破線で示した中小企業は資本金 2千万円以上 1億円未満。 「利上げ局面」は利上
げ開始から利下げ直前までの期間。右図のネット有利子負債比率は、有利子負債から現預金を控除して総資産
で除したもの。資本金 1千万円未満のデータは年度値の平均（その他は四半期値の平均） 。 
（出所）日本銀行、財務省より大和総研作成

家計部門についても、これまでのところ金利上昇の影響は限定的だ。ただし、ストック面か
ら金利上昇への耐性を見ると、借入残高対年収比率は 2000年代初めから大幅に上昇している
（図表 2左）。データが遡及可能な2002年と比べると、2026年上半期の同比率は約53％pt高い。
要因別では、借入残高の増加が同比率を約 66％pt押し上げた一方、年収の増加が約 12％pt押
し下げた。家計の借入負担はストック面で以前より重くなっており、金利上昇が長期化すれば、
その影響が徐々に顕在化する可能性がある。 
図表 2：家計の借入残高対年収比率（左） 、借入返済負担率（右）の2002年からの変化幅

（注）二人以上の勤労者世帯。左図の借入残高対年収比率は、1 世帯あたりの借入残高（「住宅・土地のための
負債」と「住宅・土地以外の負債」の合計）を年間世帯収入で除したもの。右図の借入返済負担率は 1世帯あ
たりの実収入に対する借金返済額の比率。「低所得世帯」は年間収入五分位階級のうち第Ⅰ・Ⅱ分位、「高所得
世帯」は第Ⅳ・Ⅴ分位を集計。「返済世帯比率要因」は、住宅ローン返済世帯の収入が二人以上の勤労者世帯
全体の収入に占める割合の変化による寄与を表す。 
（出所）総務省より大和総研作成

### Page 5

1

5 / 5

一方、フロー面から足元の資金繰りを見ると、収入に対する借入返済額の比率（借入返済負
担率）は、返済額の減少や収入の増加を背景に、2002年以降の低水準にある（図表2右）。2026
年上半期の借入返済負担率は2002年を約1.1％pt下回った。要因別では、住宅ローン返済世帯
の収入シェア（住宅ローン返済世帯の収入が二人以上の勤労者世帯全体の収入に占める割合）
の上昇が負担率を押し上げた一方、返済額の減少と収入の増加が押し下げた。所得階級別に見
ると、足元では低所得世帯（年収下位 4割）の返済負担率が相対的に上昇しているものの、低
所得世帯、高所得世帯（年収上位4割）ともに2002年の水準を下回る。このため、金利上昇が
家計の資金繰りを大きく圧迫する状況には至っていない。 
ただし、足元の返済負担の低さは今後も続くとは限らない。変動金利型住宅ローンでは、毎
月の返済額の急増を抑える「5 年ルール」や「125％ルール」が広く採用されている7。こうし
た仕組みは、金利上昇の影響が返済額に反映される時期を遅らせ、短期的な負担増を和らげる
半面、負担の顕在化を将来に先送りする。借入残高が高水準にあることも踏まえると、金利高
が長期化するほど家計の返済負担が高まる可能性には留意が必要だ。

中立金利を探りながら、日銀は段階的な利上げを継続へ 
当社では、日銀は次回 2026年 12月の金融政策決定会合で短期金利を1.5％に、2027年 3月
会合で 1.75％に引き上げるとみている。その後は経済・物価・金融情勢への影響を見極めるた
め、いったん政策金利を据え置くと予想する。ただし、1.75％への引き上げ後も金融環境がな
お緩和的で、物価上振れリスクが残る場合には、同年 6月会合で 2.0％への追加利上げに踏み
切る可能性がある。 
日銀は2026年3月に公表したレポート8で、6つのモデルによる自然利子率の推計レンジを▲
0.9％程度～＋0.5％程度と示した。2％の物価上昇率を想定すると、名目の中立金利は 1.1～
2.5％程度で、1.75％はそのレンジのほぼ中央に位置する。だが、中立金利を正確に推計する
ことは困難で、その推計レンジには大きな幅がある9。そのため日銀は、各種データでこれまで
の利上げの影響や物価の基調などを確認しながら、金融緩和の度合いを徐々に調整していくと
みられる。

7 5年ルールは、変動金利型住宅ローンの金利が変動しても、毎月の返済額を5年間据え置く仕組み。125％ル
ールは、返済額の見直し時に、新たな返済額の上限を従来の 125％とする仕組み。いずれも法令で一律に定め
られたものではなく、適用の有無は金融機関や商品によって異なる。 
8 日本銀行「自然利子率の動向と金融緩和度合いの評価」（「日銀レビュー」、2026-J-5、2026年3月） 
9 2026年6月会合後の記者会見で内田眞一副総裁は、中立金利の推計値には「相当なばらつき」があるとした
上で、「短期金利を上げていく過程で、それを含めた金融環境の変化ということを点検して、中立金利の水準
を探りながら、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく」と述べた（日本銀行「総裁記者会見（内田副総裁代理）」
（2026年6月16日、17日公表））。

Document 013: 1998 VS 2000，高盛主管：AI抛售更像LTCM危机而非互联网泡沫，但关键问题尚未解答

Source PDF: all report pdf/1998 VS 2000，高盛主管：AI抛售更像LTCM危机而非互联网泡沫，但关键问题尚未解答.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛 Delta One 交易主管 Privorotsky 更倾向讲本次⼤跌与 1998 年⻓期资本管理公司
（ LTCM ）危机式的流动性冲击类⽐，当时美国经济增⻓基本⾯保持完好，⼀旦流动性恢
复、美联储降息，市场迅速收复失地，但⽬前的市场没有美联储的降息护航。同时
Privorotsky 指出，当下 AI 真正的问题是营收能否最终匹配所投⼊的资本，这⼀问题尚且没
有答案。
摘要：
市场正在经历⼀场关于历史先例的激烈辩论：这究竟是 1998 年⻓期资本管理公司（ LTCM ）危机
式的流动性冲击，还是 2000 年互联⽹泡沫破裂的序幕？⾼盛 Delta One 交易主管 Rich
Privorotsky 倾向于前者，但他⾃⼰也承认，决定性答案尚未到来。
周四，纳斯达克单⽇暴跌 5.5% ，费城半导体指数重挫 12% ，标普 500 下跌 4.4% ，创下 2025 年 4
⽉以来最⼤单⽇跌幅。⾼盛波动性恐慌指数当⽇触及 10 分满分中的 9.3 分， VVIX 单⽇飙升近 45
点，创有史以来第⼆⼤单⽇涨幅，仅次于 2018 年的 " 波动末⽇ " 事件。

触发因素是⺠主党横扫选举后市场对数据中⼼建设前景的重新定价，导⽕索是德克萨斯州等多
个州对数据中⼼建设的潜在监管限制。
这场抛售引发的核⼼问题，正在成为每⼀位基⾦经理的灵魂之问： AI 投资热潮的基本逻辑是否依
然完好，还是市场正在揭⽰⼀个在经济上根本站不住脚的叙事？
程序主导的机械性抛售
周四的暴跌，从市场结构层⾯看，是⼀次机械性事件与基本⾯冲击的叠加。
Privorotsky 指出，杠杆 ETF 在半导体板块和纳斯达克 100 指数收盘前触发了机械性卖盘，叠加负
向做市商 Gamma 头⼨，共同形成 " ⾃我强化的清仓效应 " 。
野村证券的 Charlie McElligott 估算，当⽇杠杆 ETF 卖出规模达 470 亿美元，波动率控制基⾦供应
量达 610 亿美元，做市商对于标普每下跌 1% 持有约 70 亿美元的负 Gamma 敞⼝。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⾼盛美国销售交易员 Ariana Contessa 在当⽇流动与持仓报告中补充道， VIX 单⽇跳升 67% ⾄
27.77 ，⾼盛多个 AI 相关篮⼦当天创下 ChatGPT 推出以来最⼤单⽇跌幅。
⽽程序的抛售尚未结束 —— 期货收盘跌破 CTA 中期阈值 7549 点，据⾼盛下⾏ 2.5 个标准差情景测
算，未来⼀周标普 500 ⾯临逾 74.8 亿美元的程序性卖盘压⼒；瑞银则警告，⻛险控制基⾦仅当⽇
卖盘或超过 1000 亿美元。
真正的问题：买盘缺席
程序卖盘制造了价格压⼒，但 Privorotsky 认为，真正令⼈担忧的是另⼀⾯ —— 买盘的消失。
⾼盛现⾦股票部⻔当⽇净卖出 30 亿美元，其中科技股占 22 亿美元，将科技板块净卖出规模累计
推⾄ 270 亿美元，已超过 2025 年 7 ⽉那轮技术性调整的 210 亿美元。
Contessa 描述，来⾃客⼾的问询强度 " 满分 10 分 " ，但实际成交活跃度 " 仅 5 分 " ， ETF 占全天交易
量的 32% 。
尤其值得注意的是，周三曾为指数托底的超⼤型科技股（ Hyperscalers ） " 完全没有看到对应的

买盘出现 " 。
Privorotsky 的诊断直⽩⽽犀利：" 真正的问题是底层需求的缺席，尤其是来⾃⻓线基⾦的需
求 …… 感觉多策略、量化、⻓线基⾦和散⼾都在同⼀⽅向，这正是问题所在。 "
1998 还是 2000 ： Privorotsky 的倾向与保留
⾯对市场最关键的定性之争， Privorotsky 明确表达了⽴场，但并未回避内⼼的不安。
他倾向 1998 年的 LTCM 危机类⽐：俄罗斯违约和 LTCM 崩盘是当年的外部冲击，但美国经济增⻓
基本⾯保持完好，⼀旦流动性恢复、美联储降息，市场迅速收复失地。
他认为，今⽇的 AI 增⻓引擎或许同样完好，市场只是在激烈地重新定价建设融资节奏与时间表。
⽀撑这⼀判断的核⼼论据是估值与资产负债表。 Privorotsky 指出，与 2000 年不同，当前科技巨
头拥有真实的营收、现⾦流和强健的资产负债表。
⾼盛 TMT 交易员 Peter Callahan 指出，半导体指数的估值倍数相⽐ 6 ⽉⾄ 7 ⽉⾼点已压缩约 50% ，
英伟达的未来 24 个⽉滚动市盈率约为 10 倍，为 2013 年以来最低。相⽐之下， 2000 年思科的市盈

率超过 100 倍 —— 这是⽀持 1998 年类⽐最有⼒的论据。
然⽽， Privorotsky 也坦诚地列出了令他夜不能寐的另⼀⾯：

### Page 3

四⼤隐忧，市场盯错了⽅向
Privorotsky 将此次 AI 抛售背后的担忧分解为四个层次：⼀是融资能⼒不⾜以⽀撑建设规模；⼆
是营收不⾜以证明资本投⼊的合理性；三是电⼒与许可产能不⾜以⽀撑算⼒扩张；四是整体资
本开⽀轨迹不可持续。
他的判断是，市场此刻执着于第三点 —— 电⼒与许可， " 可以说是这四者中最不重要的 " 。受限的
供给并不等于需求消失，真正的问题是营收能否最终匹配所投⼊的资本。
然⽽，第⼀点 " 融资 " 正在闪烁红⾊警报。据⾼盛 IR 团队统计，超⼤型科技公司今年已发⾏ 2960
亿美元债券，⾼于⾼盛此前全年 2500 亿美元的预测，占全球所有货币投资级债券供应总量的约
9% 。
与此同时，数据中⼼信⽤违约掉期（ CDS ）周四突破 500 个基点，创历史新⾼； CoreWeave 债
券利差则超过 1200 个基点。
Privorotsky 所指出的，恰恰是⼀个结构性的恶性循环：电⼒许可的延误（第三点）正在直接推
⾼融资成本（第⼀点）。每⼀个季度的许可延迟，都意味着⼀座半建好的数据中⼼要多付⼀个季

度的利息。
政治催化剂：德克萨斯州的监管信号
此次市场抛售的直接导⽕索，是选举结果引发的监管预期转变。⾼盛估算，各州层⾯的暂停令
可能冻结约 45% 的美国计划数据中⼼产能，仅德克萨斯州便占其中约四分之⼀。
Privorotsky 认为市场对德克萨斯州候任州⻓ Gina Hinojosa 的解读过于字⾯化。他援引她的表态
——" 我们既可以推动经济发展，也可以保护德克萨斯⼈的利益，⼆者并不互相排斥 " ，以及 " 我
并不是痛恨数据中⼼ "—— 得出判断：
" 与 2000 年令⼈不安的相似之处在于资本⽀出规模之庞⼤，以及回报的⾼度不确定性。我们究竟
是在清仓⼀个健康的增⻓故事，还是在发现这个故事的经济逻辑本⾝就是错的？我倾向于前者，
但需求、利⽤率和回报必须经受住考验。 "
" 这听起来更像是监管层⾯的重置，⽽⾮意识形态上的排斥。实际⽀出或许会超出⽬前市场折价
所反映的预期。 "

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
⾼盛 IR 团队补充称，据 CNBC 报道， Hinojosa 已表⽰已取得许可的数据中⼼将被允许完⼯，
这 " 可能保护了 2027 年乃⾄ 2028 年的⼤部分建设进程 " ，⾼盛经济学家也预计相关暂停令将较为
短暂，因为数据中⼼在选⺠优先级列表中排名靠后。
但不满情绪不容忽视。据 Jefferies 援引 Heatmap 调查数据，强烈反对在居所附近建设数据中⼼的
美国⺠众⽐例，从 2025 年 9 ⽉的 24% 飙升⾄ 2026 年 8 ⽉的 61% 。
乔治亚州、俄亥俄州和密歇根州正在跟进德克萨斯州的⽴法动向。德克萨斯州的⽴法会期截⾄ 5
⽉ 31 ⽇，所谓 " 监管重置 " ，依然是⼀次真实的重置。
1998 年剧本的缺陷：当下没有美联储的降息护航
Privorotsky 以历史数据为底⽓收尾：⾃ 1950 年以来 19 次中期选举后，标普 500 在未来 12 个⽉内

均录得上涨， " 这是 12 个⽉维度的观察，不是⼀个⽉的预测 " 。他认为此类事件历史上均构成买⼊
机会，但短期内难以把握时机。
然⽽， 1998 年类⽐存在⼀个致命的内在⽭盾，⽽这个⽭盾就隐藏在 Privorotsky ⾃⼰对 1998 年的
描述之中： " ⼀旦流动性恢复、美联储降息。 "
1998 年，时任美联储主席 Greenspan 在七周内三度降息。⽽在 2026 年，市场仍在定价约 65% 的
概率押注美联储 12 ⽉加息，克利夫兰联储的 Hammack 周四上午还在公开表达维持限制性⽴场的
倾向。
这⼀次，不会有美联储召集的 LTCM 救援会议，⽽超⼤型科技公司今年已发⾏的 2960 亿美元债
券，意味着这轮建设潮对债券市场的依赖程度前所未有。
流动性⾯也并不乐观。科技股多空基⾦今年以来收益基本持平，年末有强烈动机锁定现有成
果；程序化卖盘仍在继续； Privorotsky 本⼈也坦⾔， " 真正的问题 " 在于⻓线基⾦买盘的缺失。
综合来看， 1998 年或许是正确的终点，但 2000 年可能是中途必经的路径 —— ⾄少在有实质性买

盘⼒量真正出场之前如此。唯⼀值得宽慰的，或许是这样⼀个对⽐：英伟达 10 倍远期市盈率，
终究不是思科的 100 倍以上。

### Page 5

Document 055: 2026年10月FOMCプレビュー ～インフレ認識とAI投資の持続性～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月FOMCプレビュー ～インフレ認識とAI投資の持続性～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

US Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/5 
2026 年 10 月 FOMC プレビュー 
～インフレ認識と AI 投資の持続性～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

（要旨） 
○ 10 月 FOMC（10/27～28 開催）では政策金利が 3.75～4.00％に据え置かれる可能性が高い。
FRB は 9 月に約 3 年振りとなる利上げに踏み切った一方、追加の政策調整を急がず、今後の
経済指標を見極める姿勢を示すとみられる。 
○ ウォーシュ議長の記者会見では、予想を上振れるインフレ動向に関する評価、及び一部のAI
クラウド業者による信用不安が今後の設備投資に与える影響を巡って、何らかの見解が示さ
れるかが注目される。もっとも、市場で織り込まれる12 月 FOMCの利上げを巡っては、具体
的な言及を避ける可能性が高い。 
○ FRBは5つのタスクフォースのうちの生産性と雇用（Productivity and Jobs）部会におい
て、11月16 日にサンフランシスコで公聴会を開催する予定だ。年末までに取りまとめられ
る提言や議論の進捗に関して、ウォーシュ議長から何らかの言及があるかが注目される。

### Page 2

2/5 
・据え置きが見込まれる 10 月FOMC 
2026 年10月FOMC（10/27～28開催）において、FRBは賛成多数で政策金利（3.75～4.00%）を据
え置く可能性が高い。FRBは 9 月FOMCにて米国経済の堅調な推移やインフレ高止まりを踏まえ、23
年 7 月以来となる0.25%の利上げに踏み切った。また、同時に公表されたドットチャートで年内に
追加で 1回の利上げ見通しが示された。8 月CPI及びFRBのタカ派的なスタンスを踏まえて、金融市
場は連続利上げの可能性を徐々に織り込んでいったものの、FRB高官は9月末から10 月初めにか
けてこれに慎重なスタンスを示した。例えば、9/29にウィリアムズ総裁は「急ぐ必要はなく、情
報を集める時間がある」 、10/1 にジェファーソン副議長は「 （政策判断には）もう少し時間がかか
るかもしれない」とそれぞれ述べた。 
この結果、本稿執筆時点におけるFF金利先物市場（Fed Watch）において、83.9%の確率で金利
据え置きが見込まれる一方、0.25%ptの利上げ予想は16.1%に留まる（資料1）。なお、金融市場は
12 月FOMCにおける0.25%の利上げに加えて、2027年末に向けて更に 2回程度の利上げを織り込ん
でいる（資料 2）。この場合の合計利上げ幅は+1.0%ptとなり、9月時点のドットチャート（26年に
計 0.5%ptの利上げを行い、27 年は据え置き）を上回る。もっとも、過去の利上げ局面ではそれが
1～2 回に留まることは稀であり、金融市場はこうした過去の事例も一部に織り込んでいるとみら
れる（詳細は 2026年 9月 18日付け「ＦＲＢの過去の利上げサイクルと長期金利の行方」）。

資料 1：10月 FOMCにおける利上げ織り込み 
  資料 2：市場が予想する各FOMCにおける政策金利 の確率分布（10/21時点）

出所: CME、FRB より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が作成

地区で小幅から緩やかに拡大した（2 地区がmoderate growth、3地区がmodest growth、4 地区が

### Page 3

3/5 
slight growth） 」とまとめられた。また、 「労働市場は総じて変化がなかった」と指摘した。一
方、「物価上昇は3 地区で力強く上昇し、8 地区で緩やかに上昇した。8地区のうち3地区では投
入価格の上昇圧力が強まっている」とインフレ高止まりへの懸念が強調された。また、複数の地
区でインフレによる生活費負担の増大が賃金の上昇圧力になっている可能性が指摘された。 
10 月FOMCまでには10/27に 9 月耐久財受注や10月コンファレンスボード消費者信頼感指数など
が公表されるものの、10月FOMCの政策判断に大きな影響を与える可能性は低い。

・インフレ認識、AI投資の持続性、タスクフォース 
9 月FOMCの声明文では「経済活動は堅調に拡大している」との景気認識が維持された。一方、 「イ
ンフレ率は高止まりしている（7 月時点：インフレ率は2%目標に対して高止まりしている。これは
エネルギーを含む一部セクターにおける供給ショックを通じた価格上昇を反映している） 」と、供
給ショックに関する言及が前回から削除された。10 月FOMCにおいても、こうした景気・物価認識は
概ね維持される可能性が高い。一方、物価認識に関して、足下のインフレ減速の鈍化や加速の兆し
を指摘する、或いはインフレ高止まりの背景として需要の強さに言及する場合、タカ派的なスタン
スと捉えられるだろう。 
ウォーシュ議長は記者会見において、次回 12 月FOMCに関する具体的な言及を避けるとみられる
一方、下記3 点に関して新たな見解が示されるかが焦点となろう。 
まず、足下のインフレ認識に変化があるかだ。9 月FOMCの記者会見で議長は「インフレ率は高止
まりしており、その状態はあまりに長く続いている」や「あまりに多くの品目が 3％を超えるイン
フレ率にある」と述べた。また、同会合の議事要旨では何名か（some）の参加者が「AI普及を通じ
て、需要が中期的に供給を上回り、インフレ上昇への圧力となる可能性がある」と指摘した。その
後公表された 9 月のコアCPIは予想を上振れるなど、高関税による財価格の押し上げやエネルギー
高による輸送サービスへの波及の兆しがみられる。ウォーシュ議長がこうした需要と供給双方のイ
ンフレ要因に関して、新たな見解を示すのかが注目される。 
次に、AI投資の持続性への見方だ。FOMC後の10/29に公表される7～9月期実質GDP成長率は前期
から加速すると見込まれ、AI関連を中心とした設備投資の拡大、及び資産効果（株高）による消費
の堅調さが示されるとみられる。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16 の講演において「世界経済は 5～6 か月
前に予測できなかったほどの回復力を示している」と述べ、エネルギー高や利上げに対する経済的
な耐性を強調した。一方、足下ではAIモデル開発業者などに計算資源を提供する「ネオクラウド企
業」の一部で信用不安が浮上しており、データセンターを中心としたAI関連投資が減速するとの懸
念が強まっている。ウォーシュ議長がAI投資の先行き、及び一部企業の信用不安が金融市場全体に
与える影響に関して、どのような見解を示すのかが焦点となる。 
最後に、タスクフォースの進捗状況が部分的にでも示され、2027年以降の政策運営に関するヒン
トがみられるかだ。ウォーシュ議長は就任後の6月に 5 つのタスクフォース（①コミュニケーショ
ン、②バランスシート政策、③既存データの活用、④変化する時代の生産性と雇用、⑤FRBのインフ
レ枠組み）の設置を発表し、FRB改革を推進する姿勢を示した。5つのタスクフォースは秋までに何

### Page 4

4/5 
らかの成果を得たうえで、2026 年末までにFRBへ提言をまとめる予定だ。ウォーシュ議長は 10/16
の講演にて、11 月 16 日にサンフランシスコで生産性と雇用に関する公聴会を開催すると述べてお
り、これを含むタスクフォースの進捗状況が開示されるか注目される。

「足下の地政学的な環境、 及び想定以上に強い総需要を踏まえると、
インフレリスクは上振れ方向に傾いている」と追加利上げの可能性
を指摘した一方、「（政策判断には）もう少し時間がかかるかもし
れない」と述べるなど、追加利上げを急がない姿勢を示した

ミネアポリス連銀 
カシュカリ総裁* 
米国経済と労働市場が堅調であると述べたうえで、「現状の金融政
策が景気抑制的ではない」ことを指摘した。一方、10月FOMCにおけ
る政策判断への明言は避けた

「経済指標が予想通りに推移するならば、 インフレ率を+2%に戻すた
めに追加利上げが実施されると予想する」と述べた一方、「利上げ
は連続して行う必要はない」と強調するなど、10月FOMCでは政策を
据え置くべきとの考えを示した

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告

5/5
10/12
クリーブランド連銀
ハマック総裁*
インフレ持続の背景として、企業投資の急拡大や資産効果を通じた
消費押し上げによる需要増を指摘した。こうした需要をある程度減
速させる必要性を強調したほか、投資需要を抑制することで雇用を
悪化させずにインフレを抑制できる可能性を指摘した
10/13
ニューヨーク連銀
ウィリアムズ総裁*
年内に追加で1回の利上げを支持した一方、2027年に関しては追加利
上げの必要はないと明言した。この背景として、来年はエネルギー
価格がインフレを押し上げないこと、及び関税の影響が一服するこ
とのほか、足下の米国経済が全体として過熱していないことを指摘
した
10/14
サンフランシスコ連銀
デイリー総裁

「（利上げが）必要となる可能性が高いが、必要な時間はある」と
述べるなど、10月FOMCの利上げに慎重な姿勢を示した。また、予想
を上振れた9月CPIを巡って、 「インフレリスクは依然として高いが、
リスクは現実に起きたわけではない」と、基調的なインフレ動向に
大きな変化が起きていないとの認識を示唆した
10/16 ウォーシュ議長*
AI関連を中心に「投資の急増」が生じていると指摘し、世界経済が
エネルギーショックに対する想定以上の強靭性を示しているとの楽
観的な見方に言及した。一方、先行きの政策決定には「予断を持た
ない」と述べるなど、10月FOMCに関するヒントは与えなかった
注：*は 2026 年に投票権を持つFOMC メンバー。

1 
2327 國巨(Yageo)

報告日期： 2026/10/30 研 究 員：洪瑞陽  (Ryan Hung) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 既有調價與 AI 產品出貨增加，帶動 3Q26 毛利率升至 41.0% 、
EPS 達 5.83 元；第四季雖為消費性淡季，公司仍指引營收、
毛利率及營益率小幅季增，呈現淡季不淡。  
 3Q26 AI 相關應用占營收 19.8% ，較前季 16% 提高 3.8 個百分
點， MLCC 、鉭電容與電源端產品共同帶動成長。第三季訂單
出貨比仍接近 2，公司規劃 2027 年 MLCC 及鉭電容擴產 10%
至 15% ，自第二季及下半年分批開出。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年營收 1,841.63 ／2,498.39 億元 ，YoY 增長
38.5% ／35.7% ，EPS 為 20.68 ／35.71 元。 2027 年獲利成長
來自既有調價全年化、稼動率升至滿載、擴產及高階產品組合
提升。

一、 AI 需求帶動，第四季營收與獲利率續增  
3Q26 營收 495.32 億元， QoQ 增長 11.4% 、YoY 增長 49.7% ，連續
第七季季增，創下單季營收歷史新高。價格調整逐步反映，加上
AI 產品組合與營運效率改善，毛利率達 41.0% ，較前季提高 2.5
個百分點；營業利益率 29.5% ，EPS 為 5.83 元。投入成本提高抵
銷部分效益，但營收與毛利成長仍帶動獲利增加。

AI 相關應用占營收已由 1Q26 的 15%、2Q26 的 16%， 提高至3Q26
的 19.8% 。按公布占比計算，第三季 AI 相關營收約 98.07 億元，
較第二季約 71.13 億元增長 37.9% 。成長涵蓋 MLCC 、鉭電容，以
及電源端使用的薄膜與鋁電容，反映運算、通訊與供電需求共同
增加。工業營收亦已連續七季成長，車用維持正向，消費性營收
仍有成長，但速度低於其他應用。

第四季通常為消費性產品淡季，公司考量接單及在手訂單仍強，
指引營收、毛利率與營業利益率均較第三季小幅提高。本研究採
稼動率約 95% ，以既有調價遞延反映支撐淡季營收，預估 4Q26
營收 520.09 億元， QoQ 增長 5.0% 、YoY 增長 44.6% ，毛利率
42.78% 、營業利益率 31.43% 、EPS 為 6.36 元。

二、稼動率接近滿載，新增產能 2Q27 起貢獻  
3Q26 標準型與利基型產品稼動率均接近 95% ，公司預期 4Q26 超
過 95% ，並透過去瓶頸、效率改善與逐步擴充產能支應訂單。第
投資評等：買進

2 
三季營收已較前季增加 11.4% ，訂單出貨比仍接近 2，顯示較高出
貨基期下接單維持高水準 ， 尤其MLCC 與鉭電容等 AI 相關產品需
求較強。

公司規劃 2027 年 MLCC 及鉭電容產能較目前增加 10% 至 15% ，
部分於第二季、部分於下半年開出。此為相對目前產能的整體增
幅，新增產能須隨設備交付、驗證與量產進度反映；公司亦表示，
擴產後希望稼動率回到 85% 至 90% 的健康水準。

三、漲價分批反映， 2027 年獲利仍以電容產品為主  
國巨 2026 年 2 月調高晶片電阻價格 10% 至 20%， 並同步調整鉭電
容； 7 月調價範圍擴至各產品線，假設幅度約 15% 至 25% ，並涵
蓋 EMS 與 OEM 直接客戶，通常延後一至兩季反映，因此第三季
已認列部分效益， 10 月新一季價格生效。

MLCC 與鉭電容為目前成長最明確的產品。第三季 MLCC 占營收
21.5% ，高於前季 19.1% ；鉭電容占 23.7% ，營收季增 10.0% 、年
增 45.3%。 公司表示鉭粉價格自年初趨於穩定 ， 已進行策略採購及
長期供應鏈布局，支持後續供貨與成本管理。

四、預估 2026 ／2027 年 EPS 為 20.68 ／35.71 元 
預估 2026 ／2027 年營收 1,841.63 ／2,498.39 億元 ，YoY 增長 38.5%
／35.7% ，EPS 為 20.68 ／35.71 元。2027 年隨既有調價全年反映、
假設稼動率提高至 100%，以 及MLCC 與鉭電容新增產能自第二季
起分批貢獻，帶動營收與獲利成長。

五、投資建議：買進，目標價 892 元 
參考國外 MLCC 大廠本益比，以及國巨 AI 佔比持續提高，給予
25 倍本益比，以 2027 年 EPS 計算 35.71 ，目標價 892 元，投資建
議為買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況 
一、完整被動元件產品線， AI 相關由板端延伸至電源  
國巨透過產品開發及 Pulse 、KEMET 、奇力新等併購布局，產品涵
蓋 MLCC 、鉭電容、鋁電容、薄膜電容、電阻、磁性元件與感測
器，服務工業、運算、通訊、車用及消費性市場。 AI 設備從機房
供電、機櫃電源轉換到運算板，都需要處理電壓穩定、濾波、儲
能及訊號干擾；不同位置的電壓、空間與可靠度要求不同，使完
整產品組合有利於參與客戶多個供電環節。

3 
公司第三季 AI 相關應用占比接近兩成，已分布於多項產品線。隨
運算功耗提高，成長範圍由靠近 GPU 與 ASIC 的板端電容，擴大
至通訊設備、電源模組及機櫃供電。磁性元件可參與電源轉換及
濾波，電阻用於訊號與電流相關功能，感測產品則提供溫度等監
測需求。

供應能力也逐漸成為競爭條件。國巨的多產品、全球製造據點及
既有客戶關係，有助協調產品開發與後續供貨； TDK 與太陽誘電
討論先進 MLCC 、電感的共同開發及製造、採購合作，亦反映高
容值、高可靠度與穩定供應的重要性提高。

二、MLCC 與鉭電容為主軸，高容值與穩定性優勢擴大  
AI 運算功耗與供電密度增加，要求電容在有限空間內提供足夠電
容量，並維持供電穩定。 MLCC 具備小型化與高密度配置的優勢，
對高容值、小尺寸與可靠度的要求隨平台升級提高；鉭電容則具
備高容值與電容量穩定性優勢，可配合運算及通訊設備的不同低
壓電源設計，支應儲能與濾波需求。兩者實際搭配取決於電路位
置、電壓、體積與成本。

國巨透過 KEMET 具備鉭電容與多類電容產品布局，可配合客戶
選擇不同供電方案。當高容值 MLCC 供應吃緊或設計成本提高
時，其他電容可提供設計選項，但轉換仍須完成電路調整與驗證，
不能將短缺直接換算成鉭電容新增訂單。本研究以目前已觀察到
的產品成長、擴產與價格假設推估， 2027 年 MLCC 及鉭電容營收
分別為 628.36 億元及 669.79 億元， YoY 增長 65.6% 及 51.1% ，合
計營收占比由 2026 年的 44.67% 升至 51.96% 。

4

電源端亦提供後續產品升級機會。隨資料中心朝更高功率及 800V
直流供電方向發展，不同轉換位置將提高耐壓、溫度穩定性及效
率要求。高壓端與轉換後低壓端所需元件不同，國巨可透過陶瓷、
薄膜、鋁電容與磁性元件參與多段供電。

三、日韓接單與交期延長，高階電容供需偏緊延續  
日韓大廠的出貨與接單，顯示高階電容需求已反映在營運數據。
依， JEITA 公布最新 2026 年 7 月日廠被動元件全球出貨額 4,280
億日圓，年增 23.2% ；村田因 AI 伺服器用需求急速成長，村田製
作所將擴建 MLCC 工廠，滿足需求增加。村田之前 4 月宣布，計
畫在截至 2028 年 3 月為止的 2 年 期 間，對MLCC 生產設備投資約
800 億日圓，提升 10-15% 產能。關於投資計畫，這次中島規巨表
示，「正評估能將投資時程提前多少」 。表示「原先預估位於島根
縣出雲市的工廠產能要到 2030 年才會滿載，如今卻可能提前至
2026 年就達到滿載」

根據 Future Electronics 最新資料亦顯示交期延長 。 村田低於1μF
產品交期由 9 月 26–28 週延長至 10 月 30–36 週，高於 1μF 及
車規產品由 26–30 週延長至 30–36 週； TDK 低於 1μF 產品由
26–36 週延長至 30–40 週，高 於1μF 及車規產品由 26–48 週調
整至 30–48 週。三星電機上述三類產品均由 30 週以上調整至 52
週，價格維持向上。國巨低於 1μF、高於 1μF 及車規產品交期
均維持 30–52 週，交期與價格趨勢持續向上，整體而言， AI 伺服
器與電源規格升級持續帶動被動元件需求，交期不僅維持高檔，
主要 MLCC 廠及部分電阻產品更進一步拉長，漲價品項亦有增
加，延續供不應求的正向看法。

高效能晶片的 AI 伺服器中不可或缺的元件。隨著大型科技公司準
備明年推出下一代 AI 伺服器，三星電機預計 AI 用 MLCC 的需求
將達到今年的約八倍 ， 這項預測較上一季公佈的超七倍有所上調。

國巨本次法說表示，全球代理商庫存在過去三個月由約五個月降
至四個月，大中華區代理商維持兩個月以下。供應商接單偏強、
交期延長與通路庫存下降相互呼應，增加客戶提前確保供應的需
求。

四、滿載提高既有產出， 2027 年擴產分批支應訂單

本研究預期 2027 年高階電容供需仍偏緊，主要考量需求成長、產
品升級與新增供給之間存在時間差。 AI 平台提升功耗與供電密
度，對電容量及小型化提出更高要求；高階產品也會改變產能配
置，設備投入增加未必等比例轉化為可交付的標準品顆數。三星
規劃菲律賓新廠於 2027 年量產高附加價值 MLCC，同時仍預期高
階供應偏緊，村田將兩年擴產 10-15% 計畫考慮提前，皆是供不應
求現象。

國巨新增產能自第二季及下半年陸續開出，第一季主要仍依靠既
有產能與去瓶頸。本研究採 MLCC 、鉭電容年底有效產能較目前
增加 15% ，全年平均增幅為 7.5% 。因此，年底產能增加 15% 不等
於全年供給增加 15%； 加上第三季訂單出貨比仍接近 2， 新增供給
需先支應既有接單與後續需求持續。

基於交期延長、高階產品需求增加及擴產分批到位，本研究假設
國巨於 2027 年針對 MLCC 及鉭電容有機會再調價 ， 根據今年國巨
2026 年 2 月調高晶片電阻價格 10% 至 20% ，並同步調整鉭電容；
7 月調價範圍擴至各產品線，假設幅度約 15% 至 25% ，先假設明
年特定產品再調升 15% ，並假設於下半年完整反應。此輪假設調
價僅涵蓋兩項電容，屬同規格售價提高，與既有升規組合及 2026
年調價全年化分開計算 。(註：15% 幅度及生效時點為本研究假設，
並非公司已公布指引。 )

成本方面，公司表示鉭粉價格已趨穩定，且已進行策略採購，支
持供貨及成本管理。

二、預估 2027 年營收 2,498.39 億元， EPS 為 35.71 元 
2027 年各季維持 100% 稼動率 ，MLCC 與鉭電容有效產能自第二季
起逐步增加 ， 並沿用高階產品組合提升的假設 。 第一季營收562.55
億元， QoQ 增長 8.2% ，反映稼動率由 95% 升至 100% 、調價剩餘
效益及產品組合提升。另假設第二季僅 MLCC 及鉭電容再調價
15%，第三季起完整反映，第四季維持新價格，貢獻全年新增營收
96.39 億元。第二至第四季營收分別為 587.39 、659.50 、688.95 億
元，全 年 營 收2,498.39 億元 ，YoY 增長 35.7%。 預估毛利率47.09%、
營業費用 247 億元、費用率 9.89% ，營業利益 929.55 億元， YoY
增長 77.7% ；業外淨收入 18.60 億元、基本稅率 22% ，全年 EPS
為 35.71 元， YoY 增長 72.7% 。

### Page 1

Hon Hai Precision Industry

HHTD 2026: precision manufacturing, 
vertical integration, AI DC, and new EV

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 320.00 TWD | Price: 245.50 TWD

7th HHTD: precision manufacturing, vertical integration 
Hon Hai hosted its seventh Tech Day, titled "Accelerating Intelligence" on 30 Oct. Key 
highlights included its strengths in precision manufacturing, vertical integration, robotics 
and AI datacenters, as well as the launch of the Model A+ and Model K/K+ EVs. We 
expect Hon Hai, with its strong manufacturing capability and vertical integration 
advantages, to sustain its leading position as an AI server ODM. Maintain Buy. 
Doubled AI rack production to 2k racks per week currently  
Hon Hai's Chairman indicated that the company has shortened the assembly time for a 
compute tray from 2 hours to 20 minutes. This was supported by its precision 
manufacturing capability (1µm precision), automation and a cableless design co-
developed with its customer. Amid continued strong AI demand, Hon Hai has doubled its 
AI server rack production from 1,000 racks per week a year ago to 2,000 racks per week. 
AI server solution offerings across L1-L12 and CPO 
The company can now provide up to 50% of server rack components (excluding 
GPUs/CPUs), with solution offerings spanning L1 to L12. It demonstrated its high-speed 
cables/connectors, power busbars/whips, liquid cooling solutions (manifolds/cold 
plates/UQDs), compute/switch trays including the NVIDIA Spectrum-X CPO switch tray, 
server racks, as well as SuperPOD and AI datacenter solutions. 
Robotics for factory automation and new EV 
Hon Hai showcased live demonstrations of its robotics, highlighting capabilities such as 
AI-planned assembly paths and real-time sensing and tracking. For EVs, the company 
launched Model A+ and Model K/K+. Model A+ features L4 readiness, four-wheel drive 
and a spacious cabin. Model K converts from a truck into a van with a canopy box, while 
Model K+ features a more stylish design to serve the Japanese and European markets.

Company Sector 
Electronics Manufacturing Services

Company Description 
Hon Hai is the world's largest EMS provider, with four key 
business segments - Smart Consumer Electronics, Cloud & 
Networking, Computing, and Components & Others. Hon 
Hai supplies components, final assembly, and global 
logistics for global tech giants, including Apple, Microsoft, 
Google, HP, Dell, Cisco, and Sony. Hon Hai has 
R&D/production sites globally, including Taiwan, China, 
India, Japan, Vietnam, Malaysia, Czech Republic, Mexico, and 
the US. The company was established in Taiwan in 1974.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Hon Hai as its gradual moves into 
high margin/value-added server/components/semi 
business, stronger position in AI server with rising sales, 
solid balance sheet, and attractive valuation among Greater 
China AI server players.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Hon Hai Precision Industry (HNHAF) 
We set our PO at NT$320 (15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS), at around 3SD above historical P/E 
average since 2007. We think the valuation is justified as HH's leading position in 
EMS/ODM business, improving iPhone momentum with replacement cycle, rising sales 
from AI business, more diversified global footprint than peers to mitigate 
tariff/geopolitical risk, and improving balance sheet with higher cash dividend payout.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker momentum from Apple business (iPhone, iPad 
and Apple Watch), (2) increasing competition in EMS business from peers, (3) slower 
sales/earnings delivery in auto/EV business, and (4) weaker market demand or lower 
shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better-than-expected yield rate and demand for iPhone, 
iPad and Apple Watch business, (2) further automation adoption to reduce labor costs 
and improve efficiency, (3) faster sales/earnings delivery from auto/EV business, and (4) 
stronger market demand or higher shipment from Nvidia AI server rack/system business.

Net Debt-To-Equity Ratio Net Debt = Total Debt − Cash & Equivalents Total Equity

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

1 
3006 晶豪科(ESMT)

報告日期： 2026/11/03 研 究 員：王蕎程  (Joe Wang)  
壹、訪談／法說內容 
重點摘要: 
 記憶體漲價帶動獲利： 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元， QoQ 
+58.83% ，毛 利 率77.44%；預 估4Q26 營收 239.22 億元 ，QoQ 
+8.0% ，稅後 EPS 39.50 元。  
 成熟記憶體供給吃緊： DDR2 、DDR3 合計營收占比逾五成，
受惠同業減供及客戶轉單，加上高階、商務應用需求暢旺，支
撐產品價格維持高檔。  
 預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 102.33 ／159.38 元：受惠合約
價格調升及低基期，預估全年營收分別為 672.85 ／1,019.90
億元， YoY +361.6% ／+51.6% 。

一. 3Q26 稅後 EPS 36.79 元，漲價帶動毛利率升  
晶豪科主要從事記憶體 IC 設計，產品涵蓋 DRAM、Flash、MCP 、
eMCP 及 eMMC ，今年記憶體產品營收比重約 95% 。受惠記憶體
供給吃緊、合約價格逐季調升， 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元， QoQ 
+58.83% 、YoY +519.22% ；毛利率 77.44% ，較上季增加 8.74 個
百分點；營業利益 131.50 億元， QoQ +83.33% ，營業利益率
59.37% ； 稅後純益107.59 億元 ，QoQ +82.20% ，稅 後EPS 36.79
元。營收及獲利成長主要來自產品價格上漲，出貨量受限於合作
晶圓廠產能，增幅相對有限。

二、 DDR2 、DDR3 營收占比五成，供給收縮支撐價格  
隨記憶體大廠將資源轉向 DDR4 、DDR5 及 HBM ，成熟記憶體供
給減少，缺貨情況逐步擴及 DDR3 、DDR2 及 Flash 。公司表示，
國內同業停止或減少供應 DDR2 、DDR3 ，帶動客戶轉單，第三季
兩項產品合計營收占比已超過 50% 。需求方面， PC、NB 及手機
因應 AI 應用提高記憶體搭載容量，加上 AI 基礎架構、周邊設備
與邊緣運算需求增加，有助帶動非 HBM 記憶體需求。儘管部分消
費性客戶因價格漲幅較大而觀望，高階及商務應用拉貨仍暢旺，
支撐公司接單表現。

三、 4Q26 預估營收季增 8%，aiPIM 布局邊緣 AI 應用  
展望第四季，公司表示產品價格將高於第三季，出貨量則預期與
第三季相近。考量合約價格續升，預估 4Q26 營收 239.22 億元，
QoQ 8.0% ；毛利率 78.44% ，營業利益 144.40 億元，稅後純益
投資評等：買進

四、預估 2026 ／2027 年稅後 EPS 102.33 ／159.38 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 672.85 億元 ，YoY +361.6% ；營 業 毛 利489.51
億元，毛利率 72.75% ；營業利益 370.17 億元，稅後純益 305.07
億元，稅後 EPS 102.33 元。展望 2027 年，受惠成熟記憶體供給
受限、高階及商務應用需求支撐，預期產品價格維持高檔，搭配
2026 上半年較低基期，帶動全年營運成長；惟考量代工及封測成
本逐步反映，毛利率將持平。預計 2027 年營收 1,019.90 億元，
YoY +51.6% ；營業毛利 764.21 億元，毛利率 74.93% ；營業利益
588.51 億元，稅後純益 475.16 億元， YoY +55.8% ，稅後 EPS 
159.38 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 320 元 
考量 AI 趨勢下帶動原廠將產能轉做 HBM，以 及DDR 4 轉入 DDR 
5，帶動利基型記憶體市場正向效益，記憶體供不應求的現象持
續，帶動價格持續大幅上升，使晶豪科營收獲利自 4Q25 開始明
顯跳升， 2027 年全年供需缺口無解，價格上升趨勢趨緩但仍有上
行空間，帶動晶豪科獲利維持高檔，因此，投資建議買進，目標
價 320 元(2X2027 年稅後 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
晶豪科 (3006) 成立於 1998 年 ， 公司主要以提供各類型記憶體產品
及技術為主。目前記憶體佔營收比重約 90% ，Audio 佔 10% ，其
中特定型 DRAM 產品廣泛應用於 PC 週邊 、 資訊家電 、 及消費性、
通訊等系統。公司已成功建立各種容量及介面規格的特定型
DRAM 產品線 (包括 SDRAM 、DDR I/II/III 及 PSRAM 、低耗電之
Mobile DRAM 等)；在快閃記憶體方面，亦已完成多種容量及介面
類型的 NOR Flash 及 NAND Flash 的開發，可滿足各種特定應用
系統的需求。另外，由於系統級封裝 (SiP) 之需求快速增加，晶豪
科技也已經成功開發上述各項產品所需之「良品晶粒」
(Known-Good-Die;KGD) 產品及多晶片模組封裝 (MCP) 的解決方
案，以滿足客戶的各類需求。尤於晶豪科記憶體產品大部份以利
基型記憶體為主，受標準型記憶體價格波動干擾程度相對較低，
有助維持穩定毛利率。

二.營運概況及展望

1、IoT 應用持續成長，為重要成長動能 
5G 於 2H19 正式商轉，隨著基礎建設逐漸完善，連網速度及穩定
度成熟， 2021 年進入大幅應用階段，除了 5G 智慧型手機需求量
將從 2020 年的 2～2.5 億支倍數成長至 4.5 ～5 億支之外，因 5G
高頻、高速、廣連結的特色，連網速度將較 4G 時代大幅提升 10
～100 倍，將推升物聯網 (IoT) 應用快速成長，預估 5G 同時連網
裝置可達 600 ～800 億個，較 4G 時代的 100 億個大幅成長。看好
未來物聯網市場將蓬勃發展 ， 晶豪科於2020 年新成立物聯網解決
方案部門，提供客戶 RF、Wireless 的 SoC IC 及模組提供低功率
網際網路進行裝置連結，目前使用 90nm 製程節點，未來將進入
到 55nm 。2020 年新成立的物聯網解決方案部門站營收比重僅
<0.5% ，但因全球物聯網發展將因 5G 建設而蓬勃發展，預估繼
2022 年逆勢成長後， 2023 年可望延續此一成長動能。

IOT 為晶豪科近年延伸觸角的重要產品 。 IOT 將建構於 5G 佈建的
完整性， 2020 年為 5G 建置元年， 2021 年將加速建置，加速 IOT
產業發展。由於大部份 IOT 產品僅需要 DDR 3 規格即可，且全球
主要記憶體廠均優先佈局以 Server 使用的 DDR 5 為主，因此，
預估未來 DDR 3 將保持穩定的需求量。晶豪科將以無線模組切入
中國智慧電表領域，及其他各類相關 IOT 產品，為未來營運成長
重要動能。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一. 3Q26 稅後 EPS 36.79 元，漲價帶動毛利率升 
展望第四季，公司表示產品價格高於第三季，出貨量則預期與第
三季相近 。 考量合約價格續升 ， 預估4Q26 營收 239.22 億元 ，QoQ 
+8.0%；毛 利 率78.44%， 營業利益144.40 億元 ， 稅後純益117.76
億元， EPS 39.50 元。惟晶圓代工及封裝測試成本逐步增加，後
續仍須關注售價調升與成本上漲的相對幅度。新產品方面，公司
針對邊緣 AI 運算開發 aiPIM 加速器記憶體，第三季已完成
tape-out ，後續關注晶片驗證、客戶導入及量產進度，短期營運仍
以既有記憶體產品為主要動能。。

億元，稅後 EPS 102.33 元。展望 2027 年，受惠成熟記憶體供給

Issue & News 
SK하이닉스  
(000660) 
 소음보다 방향성에 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 3,200,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.09.23) 
1,862,000 
반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.  
목표주가 (유지) 460,000원

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

软银 2032 年到期美元债单⽇下跌 0.9 美分，亚洲投资级美元债利差升⾄ 2025 年 7 ⽉以来最
宽。不过，信贷市场跌幅仍相对克制，部分机构认为当前更像拥挤交易去化与估值重置，
AI ⻓期算⼒需求尚未动摇。
摘要：
⼈⼯智能股票的抛售浪潮已从股市蔓延⾄信贷市场，软银集团⾸当其冲，旗下美元债创下近⼗
周最⼤单⽇跌幅。
软银 2032 年到期美元债周五下跌 0.9 美分，其股价盘中⼀度重挫 16% 。与此同时，亚洲投资级美
元债利差扩⼤⾄少 3 个基点，交投活跃的亚洲⾼收益债也承压明显。信贷市场的波动叠加财政⾚
字攀升、油价⾛⾼及通胀升温等多重压⼒，令本已因 AI ⽀出热潮⽽获得提振的全球经济⾯临新的
下⾏⻛险。
此番抛售是全球信贷市场更⼤范围重新定价的延续。由 AI 热潮驱动的举债狂潮已推⾼收益率，⽽
本轮股市动荡进⼀步加速了这⼀进程。据彭博汇编数据，亚洲投资级美元债平均利差周四已升
⾄ 73 个基点，为 2025 年 7 ⽉以来最宽。
信贷抛售⼒度仍逊于股市
尽管信贷市场承压，但此轮抛售的烈度明显低于权益市场。 CreditSights 分析师 Zerlina Zeng 指

出，美国国债收益率下⾏为关注总体收益率的投资者提供了⼀定缓冲。
" 美国和亚洲市场的抛售整体较为克制， "Zeng 表⽰。她补充称， 10 ⽉份的⼤幅下跌已吸引不少
投资者逢低买⼊部分亚洲⾼收益债，其中包括表现尚可的房地产信⽤债。
AI 相关压⼒向信贷传导

结。

Document 288: Apollo拟豪掷750亿美元采购AI芯片，规模远超任何一家Neocloud

Source PDF: all report pdf/Apollo拟豪掷750亿美元采购AI芯片，规模远超任何一家Neocloud.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-07
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

私募巨头 Apollo 正以 750 亿美元的惊⼈押注重塑 AI 芯⽚融资格局 ——350 亿美元购买⾕歌
TPU 租赁给 Anthropic ， 400 亿美元购⼊英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚租赁给 SpaceX 。这⼀规模
远超最⼤ neocloud CoreWeave 的 270 亿美元，使 Apollo 跻⾝仅次于亚⻢逊、微软、⾕歌的
全球芯⽚储备第四极。
摘要：
私募巨头 Apollo Global Management 正以前所未有的规模押注 AI 芯⽚市场，其两笔待完成的芯
⽚交易合计⾦额⾼达 750 亿美元，不仅远超最⼤ neocloud CoreWeave ⾃ 2023 年以来累计 270 亿
美元的芯⽚融资规模，更是排名第⼆的竞争对⼿的逾 10 倍。
据 The Information 最新报道，SpaceX 宣布将租⽤价值 400 亿美元的英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚，⽽
Apollo 将通过旗下⼦公司购⼊这批芯⽚后再租赁给 SpaceX 。此前， Apollo 已于今年 6 ⽉宣布联

合 Broadcom 与 Blackstone ，为其购买⾕歌张量处理器（ TPU ）并租赁给 Anthropic 的交易安排了
350 亿美元融资。
⼀旦上述交易完成，在 AI 芯⽚持有规模上，Apollo 将仅次于亚⻢逊、微软、⾕歌等超⼤规模云
服务商，成为全球芯⽚储备最雄厚的⾮超⼤规模玩家，彻底改变 AI 基础设施的竞争格局。 Apollo
在这些交易中通过撮合交易及管理融资基⾦赚取费⽤。
两笔巨型交易奠定芯⽚霸主地位
Apollo 当前的芯⽚布局由两笔核⼼交易构成。
其⼀是今年 6 ⽉宣布的 350 亿美元交易：Apollo 联合 Broadcom 与 Blackstone ，购买⾕歌 TPU 并租
赁给 AI 初创公司 Anthropic 。其⼆是上周随 SpaceX 公告浮出⽔⾯的 400 亿美元交易：Apollo 通过
⼦公司购⼊英伟达 Vera Rubin 芯⽚，再租赁给⻢斯克旗下的 SpaceX 。
两笔交易合计 750 亿美元，使 Apollo 在芯⽚融资领域的体量远超任何⼀家 neocloud 。据 The

Information 汇编的数据， CoreWeave ⾃ 2023 年以来通过芯⽚抵押融资累计筹资 270 亿美元，位
居 neocloud 之⾸；其后依次为 Valor （ 54 亿美元）、 Nscale （ 49 亿美元）、 IREN （ 39 亿美元）、
Lambda （ 15 亿美元）、 Crusoe （ 11 亿美元）、 Nebius （ 7.75 亿美元）及 Sharon AI （ 3.56 亿美
元）。
值得注意的是，上述统计仅涵盖以 GPU 和 TPU 为抵押的融资，不包括各家 neocloud 为运营融资
⽽发⾏的可转换债券及信贷额度。此外，上周另有⼀笔由花旗集团、美国银⾏与 Blackstone 联合
安排、规模达 630 亿美元的交易 —— ⽤于为 Anthropic 采购 Broadcom 芯⽚提供融资 —— 亦未纳⼊
上述⽐较，原因是各⽅未披露这批芯⽚的实际持有⽅。
芯⽚融资模式： SPV 结构的规模化复制
Apollo 所采⽤的芯⽚融资结构，脱胎于 CoreWeave 与 Blackstone 在 2023 年共同开创的特殊⽬的

载体（ SPV ）模式。在这⼀安排下， neocloud 与其⾦融合作⽅共同设⽴ SPV ，由 SPV 发⾏债务
以购买芯⽚，芯⽚终端⽤⼾⽀付的租⾦则⽤于偿还债务。
Apollo 的操作逻辑与此⼀脉相承，但规模更为庞⼤。与许多 neocloud 不同的是， Apollo 并未额外
投⼊资本建设⽤于存放芯⽚的数据中⼼，其商业模式更为聚焦 —— 通过购买芯⽚、出租给终端
⽤⼾，赚取交易撮合费⽤及基⾦管理费。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
20 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
图⽚
超⼤规模云服务商则⽆需借助 SPV 结构，可直接通过⾃由现⾦流、股权发⾏或传统公司债完成
芯⽚采购，这也是 Apollo 与超⼤规模云服务商之间的本质区别。
保险资本驱动 Apollo 转型
Apollo 的芯⽚豪赌，折射出这家⽼牌私募机构过去⼗年的深刻转型。 Apollo 由亿万富翁 Leon
Black 联合创⽴，成⽴已逾 36 年，早年以杠杆收购闻名，其中包括 2008 年斥资近 300 亿美元收购
赌场巨头哈拉斯娱乐。
过去⼗年间， Apollo ⼤规模吸纳来⾃保险公司的资本。保险资⾦偏好收益率⾼于投资级债券、
但信⽤评级仍维持投资级的资产类别，⽽芯⽚融资交易恰好契合这⼀需求。这⼀资本结构的转
变，为 Apollo 在 AI 基础设施领域的⼤规模布局提供了充裕的弹药。

芯⽚融资对 Apollo ⽽⾔是⼀个全新的业务⽅向，但其背后的逻辑与传统私募信贷如出⼀辙：以资
产为抵押，提供结构化融资，赚取稳定的利差收益。随着 AI 算⼒需求持续膨胀， Apollo 正将这⼀
逻辑推向新的量级。

the detail suggesting the underlying
cycle is starting to turn down.

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Total dwelling approvals rose 5.1%mth in September, while
the annual pace moved into contraction at –0.8%yr. The
result was well above both Westpac’s expectation for a
7.0%mth decline and the market expectation for a 2.0%mth
rise. Unit approvals continued to drive headline volatility,

surging in September, while detached dwellings, which
provide a better read on the underlying trend, recorded a
sizeable decline.
Note that most figures in the charts and table are presented on
a rolling-three month basis to smooth out monthly volatility.
Private detached dwelling approvals fell 5.3%mth in
September, unwinding August’s upwardly-revised 5.4%mth
rise (+1.7ppts). Recent resilience has kept the segment
elevated, with approvals remaining above 10,000 and the

annual pace at 9.6%yr. However, the quarterly profile provides
a clearer read on the underlying trend and suggests detached
dwelling approvals are beginning to respond to recent interest
rate increases (see here). Approvals rose just 0.9%qtr in
September vs 3.1%qtr in June and 5.9%qtr in March.
Private unit approvals remained volatile, rising 16.6%mth in
September following 21.7%mth decline in August. Strength
was led by high-rise approvals, while low-mid-rise approvals

recorded a more modest increase. Despite the monthly rise,
the annual pace fell further into contraction at –20.5%yr.
Among the states, NSW recorded the strongest result,
with private approvals rising 35.3%mth led by a surge in
private unit approvals. Queensland also recorded an increase
(+9.3%mth), while WA (–2.9%mth), SA (–3.5%mth) and
Victoria (–29.0%mth) all declined.
In value terms, residential building approvals fell 2.8%mth,
driven by a 3.3%mth decline in new residential approvals,

partially offset by a 0.8%mth rise in renovation activity.
Non-residential approvals once again reversed sharply, rising
45.9%mth, as the data centre-dominated ‘other commercial’
segment recorded an 8-fold increase on a non-seasonally
adjusted basis. The total value of building approvals rose
13.0%mth in September. On a non-seasonally adjusted basis,
the value of building approvals rose 8.3%mth overall, but
declined 3.0%mth when excluding the ‘other commercial’

segment.
September’s result reinforces the importance of looking
through headline volatility in unit approvals. While total
approvals rose in the month, detached dwelling approvals
slowed on a quarterly basis and fell across all states, pointing
to softer underlying momentum. The annual pace of total
approvals also moved into contraction for the first time
since January. Interest rate increases, the sharp downturn
in established housing markets and rising costs are likely to

weigh on further growth, with pessimistic consumer sentiment
an additional headwind (see here).
Detached approvals point to softer momentum

Document 320: Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-11-09)

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:04 October 23 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor
Hauser's fireside chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a follow-up rate hike at the
November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook was "materially worse" than the RBA's

### Page 2

August forecasts. Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having "crystallised" ahead of the
September meeting – the Middle East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser noted the
Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in
August”. He then positioned AI and data centre developments among an "extraordinary sequence of
positive demand shocks around the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through into

CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy Governor noted that "inflation's been coming
in at or a little bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the labour market". He went on to
state that the staff are currently assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement on
capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other words, revise up their assumption for the
NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of market pricing for a follow-up November

hike, from just a 20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition today ahead of next week's
Q3 CPI. For that release, we are forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a 0.9 (3.5%yr)
increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3
as well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast. Provided inflation prints broadly in line
with our expectations, the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.

Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3 and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to
3.7%yr. Under the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely watched measures of core
inflation took a step higher to now be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our NZ
economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October
policy review, while retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.

Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised to the upside in Q3, GDP growth accelerating from
0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on track to
achieve their 4.5-5.0% full-year growth objective.
While annual retail sales growth improved in September, rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace
of growth is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very weak versus history. Consumer
spending continues to be constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to employment

prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five years into the property downturn, house prices continue
to decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep downturn, -20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in
high-tech investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst domestically oriented businesses
as well as property’s malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and 2028 as the

contribution from net exports moderates, broad-based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect
recent targeted support measures to be broadened and given much greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr in September, exceeding both market
expectations and the Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely driven by energy, fuel
prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services

inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an immediate concern inflation, the labour market
showing resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to 4.7% in August, suggests demand

### Page 3

Related articles
risks for inflation will need close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is
released. For both events, the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The week after, the
US mid-term elections are due.

Document 342: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 9-13

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 9-13.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-07

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 9-13
Date:    3 hours ago
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 9-13

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD:
November 9-13
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 7 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Monica Sauro / Unsplash

### Page 3

The S&P 500 fell 2.7% last week, and the Nasdaq dropped 4.9%, both
posting their fourth straight week of losses. Thursday’s 4.4% plunge in the
S&P 500, its worst day since April 2025, came as investors weighed what
the Democratic sweep of Congress means for the AI data center buildout.
Both indexes rebounded Friday on strong jobs data, with the S&P 500 up
0.3% and the Nasdaq up 1.4%.
October’s CPI (Tue) and PPI (Fri) headline this week’s data calendar. Ten

Fed officials are scheduled to speak, and the UK and Eurozone report Q3
GDP. Wednesday’s Veterans Day holiday closes the bond market. Here’s
more:
(1) Earnings season. So far, 88% of S&P 500 companies have reported
their Q3 results. So 60 companies still have to report, and 13 will do so
this week. The "Earnings Squiggles" for 2026, 2027, and 2028 are likely to
rise even further over the remainder of this year (chart). Currently, industry
analysts' consensus is that S&P 500 companies’ aggregate EPS will be

$366.60 this year, $426.30 next year, and $495.10 in 2028. In the past,
Earnings Squiggles tended to fall because analysts were too optimistic.
This time, they haven't been optimistic enough!
(2) Fedspeak. Ten Fed officials are scheduled to speak this week
(Goolsbee, Barkin, Waller, Logan, Schmid, Paulson, Hammack, Daly,
Williams, and Jefferson). These are their first remarks after the
Democratic sweep. These Fed heads are likely hawkish on balance,

especially after Friday's strong employment report.

### Page 4

In early October, the federal funds rate (FFR) futures implied 3.3 rate
hikes over the next 12 months and 1.7 over the next six. Since then, those
projections have fallen to 1.9 and 1.4 (chart). Friday’s employment report
lifted the odds of a December rate hike to 78%. We think the FOMC still
needs another 50bps of increases to reach a neutral FFR.
San Francisco Fed President Mary Daly told CNBC on Friday that “the job
market remains stable and solid, but not booming,” with real wages falling

as pay lags inflation. She expects the Fed “will have to tighten policy
further.”
(3) Inflation. October’s CPI and PPI reports will be released on Tuesday
and Friday, respectively. The Cleveland Fed’s Inflation Nowcasting model
projects that the headline CPI rose 0.24% m/m and the core CPI 0.21%
m/m. That would hold headline CPI inflation at 3.7% y/y and ease core to
2.5% from 2.6% (chart). October would mark the 68th consecutive month
of CPI inflation above 2.0% y/y. The model sees both accelerating in

November, to 3.9% and 2.8% y/y.
The Nowcast also suggests that October’s PCED inflation will remain
firmly above the Fed’s 2.0% target, with the headline and core PCED
inflation rates projected at 3.8% y/y and 3.2% y/y, respectively. Both are
expected to heat up a bit in November.

### Page 5

October's PPI inflation is likely to be hotter than September's headline and
core readings of 6.3% and 5.0% (chart).
One of the more troublesome components of the PPI recently has been
transportation & warehousing services, which jumped 16.0% y/y in
September (chart). Soaring diesel prices are fueling inflation.

### Page 6

(4) ADP & unemployment claims. Payrolls rose 165,000 in October, well
above the 65,000 consensus. The unemployment rate fell to 4.1% from
4.2%. ADP’s weekly employment reading (Tue) follows a four-week
average of 16,750 for the week ended October 10, down from 17,500
(chart). ADP’s monthly report showed private payrolls up 86,000 in
October, beating the 67,000 consensus.
Initial jobless claims (Thu) follow last week's 189,000, with the four-week
average falling to 194,750, the lowest since 1969 (chart). Continuing

claims were 1,718,000 for the week ended October 23, with a four-week
average of 1,709,000.

### Page 7

(5) Global. The UK’s Q3 GDP (Thu) rose at an estimated 0.7% q/q pace,
up from 0.5% in Q2, when real GDP was up 1.4% y/y (chart).
The Eurozone’s updated flash estimate of Q3 GDP (Fri) follows the initial
0.4% q/q reading. Real GDP rose 1.3% y/y in Q3, with Spain leading the
major economies at 2.7% and France down 0.1% (chart).

### Page 8

clause.

大漲幅，高於市場預期的月增 0.5%，年增率
達6.3%，為2022年12月來最大漲幅 ，顯示
通膨壓力正從能源擴大至其他領域 。通膨僵
固性使市場預期高利率將維持更久時間 ，美
國公債殖利率一度走高 ，但隨股市重挫 、避
險資金轉入 ，公債止跌回升 。體質較弱的企
業債出現警訊 ，CoreWeave債券殖利率大幅
攀升 ， 2032 年 到 期 債 券 殖 利 率 一 度 升 至
15%，反應公司舉債擴建資料中心 ，市場擔
憂其債 務規 模 、 利 息負擔 及未來 再融 資能
力，除違約風險上升 ，過高成本侵蝕企業獲
利、股債皆挫 ，顯示目前即使是 AI、科技信
用債表現也開始出現分化。
► 中東地緣風險推升油價上漲 ，儘管川普宣布

將暫時放寬對俄羅斯柴油出口的限制 ，西德
州油價仍處於每桶 98美元高位 ，推升全球通
膨預期 。虛擬資產比特幣 、以太幣隨風險性
資產同步下挫。

► 美國9月CPI年增率上升至 3.7%，核心CPI年
增率由 2.4%升至2.6%，核心月增率 0.3%，
高於預期值0.2%。能源是通膨反彈的主因 ，
貢獻整體 CPI月增幅超過一半；能源衝擊雖
為主導 ，但消費韌性使企業保有成本轉嫁能
力，住房與部分服務價格維持黏性 ，需求面
通膨略有提高 ，加上企業資本支出提升經濟
與企業獲利韌性，使總需求不易快速降溫。
► 凱投預期Fed 12月將升息1碼，明年3月再升
1碼，政策利率可能在 4%以上維持更久 。受
到油價高盤 、通膨僵固 ，與財政惡化 、政府
發債籌資逢企業債搶錢等影響 ，美歐日主要
國家公 債利 率近 期 來到高 位 。其中 ， 法國
2027年財政預算欲刪減支出(包括養老金、公
部門工資等)引發民眾反彈 ，衝擊主權信用評
等，法國CDS與法德10年期公債利差擴大 、
歐元走弱 ，美元轉強 ，商品、黃金承壓 。法
國政治 不確定性讓 歐股修正 、法股最疲 ，成
熟國家公債殖利率升高 ，增加企業借貸成本

與股市評價壓力，續為金融市場擾動因子。

► 美國第三季財報季展開 ，市場將觀察企業在
高利率環境下的獲利表現 。根據FactSet數據
顯示 ，標普500指數第三季獲利 成長預估達
29.5%，顯示企業基本面仍具支撐 ，其中科
技 產業 受 惠AI 需求帶動 ， 獲 利 成 長預 估達
65%，持續扮演企業獲利的重要成長動能。
► 聚焦科技類股 ，AI資本支出仍為本輪企業獲
利成長的主要驅動力 。高盛指出 ，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普 500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長 ，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響 ，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間 ，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面 。隨財報
數據陸續公布 ，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現 ，並維持正向財測 ，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價 ，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。

投資焦點
► 醫療保健 、醫療服務 與生物技術等行業 ，在
景氣不好時 ，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高 ，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天 ，股市波動風險上升 ，觀察
過去當 VIX恐慌指數上揚時 ，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯 ，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率 、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑 ，部分公司估值偏低 。當
臨床數據改善 、或導入AI篩選資料與 藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值 。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力 ，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線 ，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值 ，帶動整體產業估值回升 。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場 需求，
創新藥物 (如瘦瘦針 )讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場 2030年銷售額預估上調 15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。

醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健
市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響 ，由於商業雇主
贊助保險 (ESI)和平價醫療法案 (ACA)的自付
額增加 ，以及最高自付費用要求 ，較去年同
期提高 ，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數 、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善 。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健 ，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費 ，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限 )來擴大利潤空間 ，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合 ，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險 (ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去 20 年來的最高水準 。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加 ，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑 。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長 ，市場靜待財報結果與展望 ，和對AI資本支出回收影響 。
考量地緣衝突 、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉 ，短期金融市場紛擾不止 ，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度 。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變 ，逢股市拉回時可分批布局 ，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率走揚有利銀行股 。此外，地緣衝突頻傳 ，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升 ，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間 ，殖利率偏高環境使 短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金

融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高 ，使金價短期承壓 ，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化 ，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法

### Page 17

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

Document 396: NZ labour market preview, Q3 2026 (2026-11-09)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ labour market preview, Q3 2026 (2026-11-09).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-09

### Page 1

Westpac New Zealand Economics
Turning point?
• We expect the unemployment rate to edge
down to 5.5% for the September quarter.
• Job numbers have been gaining momentum
in recent months, and now look to be enough
to outstrip population growth. However, the
participation rate will be the deciding factor.
• Despite cost-of-living pressures on workers,
the existing slack in the labour market is likely
to keep wage growth subdued.
• Our forecasts are a little more positive than

what the RBNZ assumed in their September
policy statement. Assuming they deliver on
an OCR hike this afternoon, the labour market
surveys could have an important bearing on
whether they follow up with another hike in
December.

The New Zealand economy has returned to growth over
the last year, despite a number of headwinds. That’s been
accompanied by some modest growth in jobs, though
to date it hasn’t been enough to prevent a further rise

in the unemployment rate. However, we think that next
Wednesday’s labour market reports could show the jobs
market reaching a turning point.
We expect the unemployment rate to ease from 5.6%
to 5.5% in the September quarter, which would mark
the first fall in almost five years. That’s not a firmly held
view, given the usual margin of uncertainty around this
ECONOMIC BULLETIN
Preview of Q3 labour market surveys:
4 November, 10:45am.
28 Oct 2026 | Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
measure. But we’re now seeing consistent evidence
that jobs growth is outstripping the modest pace of
population growth. We also note that the previous result
was 5.56% in unrounded terms, so it wouldn’t take much
to tip the rounded rate down to 5.5% this time.
On the whole, our forecasts are a little stronger than
what the RBNZ assumed in its September Monetary
Policy Statement. While our unemployment rate forecast

is identical, we’re more positive on both employment
and labour force participation. Meanwhile, our pick of a
0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index is slightly ahead of the
RBNZ’s estimate of 0.4%.
Assuming that the RBNZ delivers the OCR hike that we
expect this afternoon, we think they will be noncommittal
about the chances of another hike at the 9 December
Monetary Policy Statement. In that respect, the labour
market surveys could be important in setting market

expectations for the December review, as there aren’t
that many major data releases between now and then.
Forecast details.
We expect the Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS)
to show a 0.5% rise in employment for the September
quarter. That’s broadly in line with the Monthly
Employment Indicator (MEI), with the September release
this morning showing a third straight month of growth
in filled jobs. We note that employment growth in the
HLFS has actually outstripped the MEI for the last nine

quarters, but it’s not clear why this has been the case and
we’re wary of extrapolating it too far into the next quarter.

Employment growth of around 0.5% would be more than
enough to absorb the modest 0.3% rise in the working-
age population, and on its own would suggest a fall in

the unemployment rate. But it also comes down to the
rate of labour force participation, which we don’t really
have much guidance on. The participation rate has been
choppy over recent quarters, and in the June quarter
we saw an unusually large 0.3pt rise to 70.7%; we’re
assuming some payback this time with a more modest
0.1pt rise to 70.8%.
We’ll also be keeping an eye on the broader indicators of
slack in the labour market, although we don’t formally use

these in our forecasts. The June quarter saw a sharp rise
in the underutilisation rate from 12.9% to 13.8%. However,
we think there’s some residual seasonality in this series,
relating to one component in particular: people who are
available for work but not actively looking. In that case,
we could see a substantial pullback in the underutilisation
measure this time.
Previously we’ve characterised the labour market as a
“low-fire, low-hire” environment, with much of the rise in

unemployment reflecting the difficulty of breaking into
the workforce, rather than job losses. There are some
signs of a thaw emerging, with job vacancies picking up
from their lows over the last year and business surveys
pointing to a lift in hiring intentions. But we still expect
that it will be a long road to recovery – our current
forecasts see the unemployment rate at 5% by the end of
next year.
While cost-of-living pressures are again rearing their

head as a major concern for workers, the existing degree
of slack in the labour market will continue to make it
difficult for them to push for bigger pay increases. We
expect a 0.5% rise in the Labour Cost Index (LCI) for
the September quarter, which would nudge the annual
growth rate up slightly to 2.1%. That said, we don’t have a
lot to go on for this forecast. The MEI includes a measure
of median weekly earnings, which has shown no sign of
acceleration so far – but this is only a very loose indicator

of wage pressures, as it will also reflect any changes in
the composition of jobs and pay rates.

Document 444: Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed

Source PDF: all report pdf/Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs (Top of Mind)
Date: 2026-08-25

Status: ok

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less transparent era. So, is that
a positive or negative development, and what could it mean for markets and the
economy? We speak with Former Fed governors Donald Kohn and Stephen Miran
as well as GS’ Jan Hatzius who agree that rigid “Odyssean” forward guidance that
commits policymakers to a pre-set path risks policy mistakes and is therefore rarely
appropriate. But they somewhat disagree about the benefits of great er transparency

around the Fed’s reaction function. Hatzius and Kohn argue that such transparency
helps markets better anticipate and price in policy changes, accelerating monetary
policy transmission. But Miran believes that the line between committing to a path
and being transparent is clearer in theory than in practice. And while GS economists
and strategists find that reduc ed transparency leads to increased market volatility,
the question is whether this just increases the market’s noise or also improves the

market’s signal. Hatzius argues largely for the former and Miran for the latter.
Jan Hatzius, Chief Economist and Head of Global Investment Research, Goldman Sachs
“Markets price what they think the Fed will do, not what they think the Fed should do. That will remain true even if the Fed
obscures its reaction function… market guesses about the Fed’s next moves will simply become worse, leading to more
volatility... and importantly, volatility that serves no constructive economic purpose.”

Donald Kohn, former Vice Chairman of the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System, Robert V . Roosa
Chair in International Economics and Senior Fellow, Brookings Institution
“I sympathize with Chairman Warsh to some extent. [But] the real problem with Warsh’s approach is his reluctance to articulate
how the FOMC sees the economy evolving… explaining the Fed’s thinking about the economic outlook is essential in helping
markets act as a stabilizing force and is a core component of accountability.”

### Page 3

El
Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research 3 
Top of Mind Issue 151

New Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less 
transparent era characterized by shorter post-meeting 
statements, less forward guidance, and a more limited role for 
projections, marking a meaningful break from the 
transparency revolution that has defined central banking over 
the last quarter century. So, what could a less transparent Fed 
mean for markets and the economy? 
For answers, we turn to former Fed governors Donald Kohn, 
now Robert V. Roosa Chair in International Economics and 
senior fellow at the Brookings Institution, and Stephen Miran, 
now senior strategist at Hudson Bay Capital Management, as 
well as Jan Hatzius, GS’ Chief Economist and Head of Global 
Investment Research. 
We first ask whether scaling back Fed communication is a 
positive or negative development. Hatzius says it’s likely 
negative, though he draws a clear line between transparency 
and forward guidance. He argues that clarity about the Fed’s 
reaction function helps markets better anticipate policy 
changes and quickly incorporate new information into financial 
conditions, thereby accelerating monetary policy transmission 
to the economy. So, he believes that unwinding that 
transparency would be a mistake. (Note: GS Global 
Economists Joseph Briggs and Megan Peters lay out the 
evidence backing this argument on pgs. 14-15.) 
But Hatzius takes a more nuanced view of forward guidance, 
arguing that rigid “Odyssean” guidance that commits 
policymakers to a pre-set path regardless of new information 
only makes sense at the effective lower bound for the policy 
rate, while conditional “Delphic” guidance tied to the 
economic outlook is generally more defensible. Although 
markets could confuse the two, he believes that most 
sophisticated investors understand the difference and is 
therefore not particularly worried that markets have become 
overly reliant on the policy dots published in the Fed’s 
Summary of Economic Projections (SEP). Instead, he 
considers them a “useful source of additional information 
about the Fed’s reaction function” (except for the long-run 
policy dot). If anything, Hatzius would prefer the dots move 
in an even more informative direction—by publishing each 
policymaker’s combined projections for growth, inflation, 
unemployment, and the policy rate on an anonymized basis. 
Kohn agrees that clarity about the Fed’s “narrative” and 
reaction function is vital. Explaining how the Fed sees the 
economy evolving and how policy would respond, he says, is 
“essential in helping markets act as a stabilizing force and is 
a core component of accountability.” Still, he sympathizes 
somewhat with Chairman Warsh’s view that the dot plot is 
“not helpful” (see pg. 9 for Warsh’s own words on this), 
arguing that the market’s hyper-focus on the SEP’s median 
policy dot “illustrates the risk of excessive precision and 
false certainty,” as small shifts in it can trigger unwarranted 
market moves. So, he has long favored omitting the median. 
Miran, though, is more supportive of Warsh’s push for a less 
transparent Fed. Like Hatzius and Kohn, he’s concerned that 
excessive forward guidance that locks the Fed into a policy 
path raises the risk of policy errors and also argues that such 
guidance can impair market pricing of risk. But he’s less 
convinced about the benefits of increased transparency 
around the Fed’s reaction function, making the case that the 
distinction between the Fed committing to a pre-set policy 
path and being transparent about its reaction function is clear 
in theory, but less so in practice. So, he thinks “the policy dots 
absolutely have to go” but feels less strongly about omitting 
the SEP’s economic projections. 
But would less transparency allow markets to provide the Fed 
with more “direct and unfiltered” information about the 
economy, as Warsh has argued? The answer seems to boil 
down to the implications for market volatility, and whether any 
resulting volatility would simply increase the market’s noise or 
also improve its signal. 
As for the volatility implications, the evidence is fairly clear. 
Briggs and Peters find that increased central bank 
transparency has led to a reduction in market volatility over the 
last several decades. This is consistent with GS Head of US 
Rates Strategy William Marshall’s recent observation of the 
reverse: that the US rates market has already priced 
substantial uncertainty associated with a less transparent Fed, 
with the July FOMC representing the largest non-cut meeting 
day surprise in almost three decades.  
But Marshall argues that whether such meeting-day surprises 
lead to structurally higher rates volatility depends (again) on 
whether the Fed’s reaction function is well understood. If it’s 
not, he would expect higher rates volatility to persist. GS FX 
Strategists Michael Cahill and Lexi Kanter find a similar 
pattern in FX: that central bank communication shifts can 
change which events and data drive FX volatility without 
necessarily raising it, but less transparency could increase 
Dollar volatility—all else equal (though global macro 
conditions remain its biggest driver).  
But the key debate seems to be whether the increased 
volatility resulting from less Fed transparency will improve or 
worsen the market’s signal. Hatzius is clearly in the “worsen” 
camp. He argues that markets price what they think the Fed 
will do—not what it should do—and less transparency won’t 
change that; it would simply produce worse guesses. So, he 
says that while volatility in and of itself is not necessarily bad, 
volatility driven by uncertainty about the Fed’s reaction 
function would serve no constructive economic purpose. 
Kohn agrees that the lack of a clear Fed narrative could create 
damaging uncertainty. But he argues that removing overly 
specific guidance could help free markets to process new 
information more independently and isn’t too worried about 
modestly higher volatility as a result. And Miran seems most 
convinced that reducing transparency would improve the 
markets’ signal, with volatility an unavoidable but acceptable 
cost: “The first best world of signal without volatility doesn't 
exist. I'd rather have that signal, even if it's messy, than 
throw the signal out by anchoring market expectations to the 
Fed's near-term projections.” 
Allison Nathan, Editor 
Email: allison.nathan@gs.com   
Tel: 212-357-7504   
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC    
Assessing less Fed transparency

### Page 4

El
Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research 4 
Top of Mind Issue 151

Jan Hatzius is Chief Economist and Head of Global Investment Research at Goldman Sachs. 
Below, he argues that a shift toward a less transparent Fed is likely a negative development.  
Ashley Rhodes: Fed Chairman Kevin 
Warsh appears to be ushering the 
Fed into a less transparent era. Is 
that shift a positive or negative 
development? 
Jan Hatzius: It’s likely a negative 
development, although I would 
distinguish between transparency and 
forward guidance. The transparency 
revolution in central banking over the last 30 years has been 
extremely successful. When a central bank provides 
meaningful information about its reaction function—how it’s 
likely to respond to different economic outcomes—markets can 
better anticipate policy changes. So, new information gets 
incorporated quickly into financial conditions, accelerating the 
transmission of monetary policy. The central bank can later 
decide whether to ratify that move and always retains the 
option not to. That framework has worked well for the Fed over 
the past several decades. Unwinding transparency about the 
reaction function risks delaying the transmission of monetary 
policy and would be a mistake, in my view. 
Forward guidance about the policy rate path is a separate and 
more nuanced issue. In assessing the pros and cons, it’s 
important to distinguish between “Odyssean” and “Delphic” 
forward guidance. With Odyssean guidance, like Odysseus 
tying himself to the mast, the central bank commits to a policy 
path even if subsequent data point in a different direction. That 
only makes sense at the effective lower bound for the policy 
rate, when conventional monetary easing is no longer an 
option. Even then, the hurdle for Odyssean guidance should be 
high because there is a real risk that policymakers lock 
themselves into an inappropriate policy stance. By contrast, 
Delphic guidance, named for the Oracle of Delphi, is a 
conditional description of what policymakers expect to do given 
their economic outlook that will change with new information. 
Delphic guidance is more defensible, even in normal times. 
Ashley Rhodes: Let’s take these in turn. Do you agree with 
the argument that Odyssean forward guidance increases 
the risk of policy errors? 
Jan Hatzius: That argument is a serious one. In terms of 
practical examples, it is heavily centered on the 2021–2022 
episode, when the Fed tightened policy much too late because 
they made a major forecasting error on inflation at a time of 
rigid Odyssean forward guidance. That was a bad combination. 
If the forecast is wrong, policy will be set incorrectly under any 
communication or transparency framework. And Odyssean 
forward guidance compounds that problem because it delays 
the inevitable course correction. This is why Odyssean forward 
guidance should be used sparingly, if at all. 
That said, I am not sure how big a difference to the inflation 
outcome or to economic performance more broadly it would 
have made if the Fed had started to tighten in, say, September 
2021 rather than March 2022. By early 2022, markets had 
already recognized that the funds rate would need to rise, and 
financial conditions tightened substantially on the back of 
higher rate expectations well before the Fed delivered those 
hikes. Also, the error was ultimately not disastrous 
economically; inflation expectations did not de-anchor and the 
Fed course-corrected without causing a recession. 
Ashley Rhodes: Even if the Fed just provides Delphic 
guidance, are concerns that the market might confuse it 
with Odyssean guidance warranted, especially given how 
much weight markets put on the dot plot? 
Jan Hatzius: Such confusion would be problematic, but I think 
it doesn’t happen frequently in practice. Most sophisticated 
observers understand that the policy dots included in the 
Summary of Economic Projections (SEP) are conditional on data 
evolving in line with the economic projections. And the dots 
only provide information about the individual and anonymized 
views of FOMC participants, including non-voters. So, they 
aren’t strong forward guidance, not even strong Delphic 
forward guidance. And market expectations have often 
diverged meaningfully from them, undermining the argument 
that investors view them as a commitment.  
Instead, the dots are a useful source of additional information 
about the Fed’s reaction function. The SEP could be made even 
more informative by publishing each policymaker’s combined 
projections for growth, inflation, unemployment, and the Fed 
funds rate on an anonymous basis, which we have long argued 
for. Doing so would provide a clearer picture of how 
policymakers’ economic forecasts relate to their anticipated 
policy paths. But that seems unlikely given Warsh’s apparent 
preference for moving in the opposite direction. 
Concerns about market confusion are more understandable 
when it comes to the long-run policy dot, where the Fed has 
less information to convey. Nobody knows where R-star is—as 
Chair Powell liked to say, “it is seen by its works”—but 
markets seem to assume that the Fed has special knowledge 
and therefore place too much weight on the long-term dot. 
That can lock down longer-term rate expectations too firmly. 
That said, removing the long-run dot could complicate the Fed’s 
communication in its current form, as it has become an 
important benchmark for assessing the stance of monetary 
policy. I would prefer to eliminate the long-run dot and 
communicate the stance of policy with greater emphasis on 
financial conditions. Overall, though, the dot plot works well, 
and, despite the controversy surrounding it, I remain a fan. 
Ashley Rhodes: Aren’t the Fed’s inflation and employment 
targets sufficient for the market to infer the Fed’s reaction? 
Jan Hatzius: No, because getting from point A to point B 
requires more information than just a stated objective. Take the 
inflation target, for example. The Fed's goal is 2% inflation, but 
how do you assess whether inflation is moving sustainably 
toward that target? How do you determine the current 
underlying rate of inflation? Should you look at headline PCE, 
core PCE, trimmed mean inflation, or something else entirely? 
Do you project future inflation changes based on the 
Interview with Jan Hatzius

### Page 5

El
Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research 5
Top of Mind Issue 151
unemployment rate via a Phillips curve relationship, the
monetary aggregates, or something else? To anticipate the
Fed’s moves—and thereby push financial conditions in a
stabilizing direction—markets need insight into how the Fed
assesses these questions. Those considerations go well
beyond stating a broad policy goal.
Ashley Rhodes: Is there any merit to communicating less
given that Fed officials have, at times, inadvertently made

market-moving remarks?
Jan Hatzius: There is certainly an optimal level and frequency
of communication. The Fed should not provide running
commentary on every data release, especially as markets tend
to overemphasize individual data points relative to the longer-
term trend. The Reserve Bank presidents speak a lot more
frequently than the governors and their comments are almost
by definition less informative about the policy path than those
of the Fed leadership. While that can complicate the public's

understanding of the policy outlook, it can also be informative
for market participants who nevertheless understand that the
views of the troika—the Fed chairman, Fed vice chair, and New
York Fed president—carry the greatest weight. On net, it’s
therefore unclear whether noise from frequent policymaker
comments has had big practical costs. More broadly,
communication mistakes are inevitable, and policymakers will
say things they later wish they hadn't. But the question is

whether these occasional mistakes outweigh the benefits from
transparent communication. In my view, they don’t.
Ashley Rhodes: Warsh believes that providing less
guidance about the Fed’s reaction function will allow
policymakers to draw more “direct and unfiltered”
information from markets. So, you don’t agree?
Jan Hatzius: I don’t. Markets price what they think the Fed will
do, not what they think the Fed should do. That will remain true
even if the Fed obscures its reaction function. So, if the Fed

chooses to do so, market guesses about the Fed’s next moves
will simply become worse, leading to more volatility in rates
markets and in financial conditions—and importantly, volatility
that serves no constructive economic purpose. Volatility is not
bad in and of itself. For example, if new data shows increased
inflation risks, financial conditions should tighten as markets
price in rate hikes. This accelerates the transmission of
monetary policy. But volatility driven by uncertainty about the

Fed’s reaction function is more random and doesn’t necessarily
push financial conditions in a stabilizing direction.
Also, policymakers can already extract valuable information
from market indicators. For example, a sharp rise in breakeven
inflation above target is a statement that markets view the
expected policy path as inappropriately loose. So, the
information is already available without the Fed saying less.
Ashley Rhodes: Is there a risk of a “hall of mirrors” effect if

the Fed uses market prices to not only glean information
about the economy but also to guide what it should do?
Jan Hatzius: Yes, but I am not particularly concerned about
this risk. While a “hall of mirrors” effect—whereby markets
watch the Fed for clues, but the Fed watches the markets for
clues—could push the policy stance far away from desirable
levels, the likelihood of that scenario materializing is low.
Warsh has said that he does not want to “be constrained or

take verbatim from what the market’s doing.” And Fed
officials, including the Chairman, are unlikely to take that logic
to its extreme conclusion and follow the market simply
because it has priced in a particular policy move.
Ashley Rhodes: Warsh has also said that financial markets
“are probably the most important source of information to
guide central bankers.” Do you agree?
Jan Hatzius: Not entirely, as I would rank the economic data
first. That said, financial market prices are very important, and

central banks have become increasingly sophisticated in
extracting information from them. There’s always room for
improvement, but again, the information is already available
under the current regime. And communicating less
transparently wouldn’t help policymakers evaluate financial
market signals more appropriately.
Ashley Rhodes: How durable is a less transparent Fed?
Jan Hatzius: That remains an open question. As Chairman,
Warsh has significant control over the Fed’s communications

framework and can choose to provide less information at press
conferences, as he has done. In that sense, a less transparent
approach could prove durable, though its consequences remain
to be seen. Other communication changes, such as eliminating
the dot plot, would probably in practice require FOMC support,
and my sense is that other Fed officials would see that as too
great a step away from transparency.
A large reduction in Fed communication, defined by the

number of speeches, is less likely to endure. Reserve Bank
presidents have strong incentives to continue talking, in part
because the influence and relevance of their institutions
depend on it. This has been a perennial issue—Chairman Alan
Greenspan was often very frustrated with the Bank presidents.
Warsh could try to rein them in. But even Greenspan in his
heyday failed to do so. If regional bank presidents continue
speaking frequently while centralized communication declines,

the cacophony could actually worsen.
Ashley Rhodes: More broadly, while the Fed held rates
steady in July, three FOMC participants dissented in favor
of a hike, fueling debate about whether further tightening
is on the horizon. Where do you stand?
Jan Hatzius: We don’t expect the Fed to hike, largely because
we think much of the recent strength in core PCE inflation
reflects temporary factors: higher oil prices related to the US-
Iran war, roughly 70bp of lingering tariff pass-through, and

distortions in software and accessories prices. As these effects
fade, we expect core inflation to move close to 2% in 2027.
That assessment is consistent with the ongoing deceleration in
other measures of underlying inflation such as trimmed-mean
PCE and core CPI.
The recent data are also broadly consistent with that view. The
inflation data improved significantly in June and July, and the
labor market reports for both months were somewhat weaker

on balance. If those trends continue, policymakers will likely
conclude that additional tightening is unnecessary, especially
with the funds rate already around 3.5-3.75%—a level the Fed
likely views as roughly neutral or even slightly restrictive.
